### `coverage`

FHIR resource `Coverage`. US Core Coverage: a patient's insurance, in priority order (``order``). Member ID is an identifier (type MB); group number and plan name are coverage_class rows. Card images (front / back) are coverage_card_image rows. entry_source 'ai-generated' when captured by card OCR (P1). At most one active coverage per patient per order.

Patient-owned via `beneficiary_patient_id` (same-patient FKs apply).

### `detected_issue`

FHIR resource `DetectedIssue`. A CDS alert shown at prescribing / ordering time: allergy, drug-drug interaction, duplicate therapy, dose range (layer 1, deterministic) or an AI suggestion (layer 3). Overrides are mitigations with a reason. A high-severity alert on a request that becomes active must have a mitigation (deferred trigger).

Patient-owned via `patient_id` (same-patient FKs apply).

### `medication_request`

FHIR resource `MedicationRequest`. US Core MedicationRequest (a prescription). Beyond draft it must be signed by a practitioner; once signed its clinical content is frozen (trigger) and every version is in fhir_resource_history. Amendments are a new request with priorPrescription. AI output can never become a prescription without the signing clinician (FR-MED-02).

Patient-owned via `subject_patient_id` (same-patient FKs apply).

### `observation`

FHIR resource `Observation`. US Core Observation profiles: vital signs (BP as components), laboratory results, smoking status and other social history, surveys, exam findings, point-of-care tests.
